# NEXORA — Continuous Development Work Queue

**Owner order (2026-11-20 23:48 Dhaka):** Keep developing continuously, night and day, until the owner says stop. Merge to GitHub as each unit completes. No per-unit permission needed for work inside this queue's scope.

**Scope limits (never cross, even under the standing order):**
- Only the NEXORA project: repo `zobaerio/Nexora`, local tree `~/workspace/gta-style-game/`
- Preserve the Godot testbed; never delete or overwrite existing merged work
- UE5 code written in this VM is **uncompiled scaffolding** — label it so in every file header and PR body; never claim a UE feature works without a build on the owner's PC
- No other repos, no production systems, no purchases, no external messages
- If a GitHub approval card times out or a push/merge fails twice on the same unit: commit the work locally, note it in `STATUS.md`, and move to the next unit — never force-push, never retry in a loop

**Unit workflow:** local files → verify content (read back / JSON-validate / re-run Godot tests if touched) → commit locally → mark unit DONE here + in `STATUS.md`.
**GitHub batching rule (owner, 2026-11-20 23:53 — he has given FULL permission and is frustrated by approval cards, which are per-write by platform design and cannot be disabled):** batch all units completed in one work session/run into ONE branch `feat/<run-slug>` → one push-files → one PR (not draft) → one squash merge → read-back verify one key file per unit on `main` (ref=refs/heads/main). If a card times out, the local commits stand and the next session's batch includes those files (check main first).

# Queue 4 — ordered by the owner ("Next", 2026-11-21 07:48)

Fix the four economy findings from Unit T's simulator report (`docs/ECONOMY-SIM-REPORT.md`). Data changes in U–W are authorised by this queue; each must be justified by its finding and proven by the Unit X re-run. Same scope limits, honesty rules and GitHub batching rule as Queues 1–3.

- [x] **Unit U — Taxi Rental Fix (Finding 1)** (`data/prices.json` + GDD-03 §3.3/§8 + GDD-07 §2 + `data/vehicles.json` note): entry taxi renting loses ~13,700/mo (rent 800/day vs 14,000 gross) — the designed rent-before-you-buy on-ramp is broken. Lower the daily cab rental so renting ends modestly positive, clearly worse than owning but survivable; update every document that states the old number.
- [x] **Unit V — Senior Cost Scaling (Finding 2)** (`data/prices.json` + GDD-03): senior tiers save 71–84% because living costs are flat while wages scale. Introduce tier-scaled living costs (eating-out/services lines + a lifestyle-uplift mechanism owned by the data) so senior savings land roughly in the 35–55% band; document the reasoning.
- [x] **Unit W — Per-Profession Starting Balances (Finding 3)** (`data/professions.json` + GDD-03): monthly-paid professions dip negative in month 1 from a zero start (pilot −34,800). Add `starting_balance_ncr` per profession (civil engineer keeps the canonical 25,000), sized from the simulator's month-1 dip so no profession goes negative on its normal path.
- [x] **Unit X — Simulator v2 + Full Re-run** (`tools/economy_sim.py` + `docs/ECONOMY-SIM-REPORT-V2.md`): model business-owner/shop-owner rows with real operating costs (full P&L + 8% business tax — v1 treated their bands as pre-cost upper bounds, Finding 6), apply U–W, re-run everything deterministically, and publish the v2 report with verbatim output + a before/after verdict per finding (fixed / improved / still open, honestly).

# Session 01 — Autonomous Human Intelligence & Life Simulation Engine (owner order "BEGIN SESSION 01", 2026-11-21 08:30)

**STANDING GATE RULE (owner, 2026-11-21 08:32):** a later Session's implementation NEVER starts until the current Session's every unit is genuinely implemented AND tested with acceptance criteria passing. **Session 02 (Family, Society & Social Civilization Engine, 12 units) is QUEUED/LOCKED behind Session 01** — its spec is on record with the owner; no Session 02 work may begin until Session 01 completes under this rule.

**Strict completion rule (owner):** no unit counts as done without functioning logic, persistent data, and verified simulation behaviour proven by automated tests. **Implementation form:** a real, runnable, deterministic Python simulation core at `tools/sim/` (stdlib only), SQLite persistence mirroring `data/schema.sql` (the PostgreSQL file remains the Phase 9 server schema and is NOT rewritten), population/world data only from the merged files. This session is also the audit's **Unit AD — Headless Simulation Core v1**; its 9 completion criteria are adopted into the acceptance suite. The UE layer is untouched by this session — the sim is the headless core the UE/save design maps onto.

- [x] **Unit 01 — Human Identity Engine** (`tools/sim/identity.py`): persistent unique identities (Human ID, names, derived age, birthplace, nationality, family background, goals), profile card read from live rows.
- [x] **Unit 02 — Personality Engine** (`tools/sim/personality.py`): 12-attribute profiles, GDD-01's 5 canonical traits derived by fixed mapping, profession affinities, clamped drift, compatibility.
- [x] **Unit 03 — Human Memory Engine** (`tools/sim/memory.py`): episodic memory (Full tier, cap 200, weakest forgotten first), semantic memory on relationship edges, world life-event log.
- [x] **Unit 04 — Daily Life Engine** (`tools/sim/scheduler.py`): GDD-06 template instantiation, fixed per-NPC jitter, Friday/Saturday weekend rules transcribed from the data's notes, needs bending, weather interrupts, rest days; full-day coverage invariant.
- [x] **Unit 05 — Human Needs Engine** (`tools/sim/needs.py`): 10 needs as satisfactions driven by activity, meals, and daily economic facts.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Emotional Simulation Engine** (`tools/sim/emotions.py`): 9 emotions, event-driven deltas only, personality baselines + sensitivity scaling.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Family & Relationship Engine** (`tools/sim/relationships.py`): persistent directional edges, semantic flags, marriage + household merge, childbirth, weekly drift.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Human Decision-Making Engine** (`tools/sim/decisions.py`): utility decisions from personality+needs+emotions+memory+resources+goals; every decision logged and changes real state (employment, promotion, business founding, home purchase, marriage, children, socialising, rest).
- [x] **Unit 09 — Life Cycle Engine** (`tools/sim/lifecycle.py`): derived life stages, transitions, retirement at 65, death with the full GDD-01 §6.4 inheritance cascade.
- [x] **Unit 10 — Human Interaction Engine** (`tools/sim/interaction.py`): co-located encounters by zone/anchor/time — bonding, friendship formation, partnership formation, conflicts, household family time.
- [x] **Engine + population + acceptance** (`tools/sim/engine.py`, `population.py`, `persistence.py`, `__main__.py`; tests in `tools/sim/tests/`): District 1 starter population from merged data only; tick = block boundaries + daily rollover; monthly settlement reconciling with economy_sim v2; 53 automated tests (per-unit + acceptance + no-regression) all passing; report at `docs/SESSION-01-REPORT.md`.

**Session 01 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — all 10 units implemented and test-proven; the Session 02 gate is now OPEN under the owner's standing rule (Session 02 itself starts only on the owner's order).

- [x] **Unit 12 — Generational Development** (`tools/sim/generations.py`): derived generation index + lineage, persisted generational history (births, inheritances, knowledge transfers), elder→child teaching through capped accrual.

# Session 04 — Autonomous Economy & Global Business Simulation Engine (owner order "BEGIN SESSION 04", 2026-11-21)

**Gate check:** Session 03 completed + merged (PR #14, main `3da07059`, 183 tests) before this session began, per the standing gate rule. **Implementation form:** extend the existing economy — the ledger is the single traceability spine (idempotency keys + event refs + business endpoints + append-only triggers added in place); `tools/economy_sim.py` and `data/schema.sql` untouched; SQLite extensions additive only (15 new tables). **Owner's special order:** every unit of money traceable — source, destination, causing event. Full detail: `docs/SESSION-04-REPORT.md`.

- [x] **Unit 01 — Economic Foundation** (`tools/sim/finance.py`, `data/economy-config.json`): two cities as regions of one national economy, characteristics derived from records; NCr currency as configuration (asserted against prices.json); event history = business + city event logs.
- [x] **Unit 02 — Individual Finance** (`tools/sim/finance.py`): per-person profile computed from the ledger (income/expenses by reason, assets, debt, obligations, purchasing power, net worth); impossible transactions rejected by construction (atomicity test-proven).
- [x] **Unit 03 — Labour Market** (`tools/sim/labour.py`): vacancies/applications/hiring/firing/resignation; business employers pay from their own accounts at settlement (shortfalls recorded); insolvent employers cannot post; credential gates reused; unemployment measured per city + national.
- [x] **Unit 04 — Businesses** (`tools/sim/business.py`): real entities in real commercial buildings (all leasable units materialised as commercial properties); owner firms + labelled system-founded firms seeded in proportion to current households; capital via the ledger; rent (system firms only), monthly P&L, distress → closure with layoffs + liquidation, all recorded.
- [x] **Unit 05 — Production** (`tools/sim/market.py`, `data/production-recipes.json`, `data/economy-goods.json`): 6 recipes over 6 goods consuming real inventory + crew, materials paid from real accounts; storm halving; producers idle at full storehouses.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Supply Chain** (`tools/sim/market.py`): orders toward recorded-demand targets over the Session 03 road graphs, shipping per unit-km; pass-start stock pool (downstream-only, no circulation); 50%-of-balance order budget cap.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Markets & Pricing** (`tools/sim/market.py`): dynamic layer over prices.json base prices; coverage-driven bounded steps inside a floor/cap band; every change persists its signal values in `price_history`.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Banking** (`tools/sim/banking.py`): system bank lending only what it holds; accounts layered on the ledger (reconciliation identity tested); credit assessment from real wage history; design loan/mortgage/savings terms from prices.json; amortising repayments, misses → default → mortgage foreclosure.
- [x] **Unit 09 — Investment** (`tools/sim/banking.py`): equity in real businesses at a documented valuation (cash + inventory at market prices, 100 shares); dividends from recorded profit at a labelled 30% payout; bonds/funds not modelled (no honest issuers).
- [x] **Unit 10 — Government Finance** (`tools/sim/govfinance.py`): tax-rules view referencing prices.json (never copying); sales tax 5% as a purchase split + import tariff 5% (SIM MODEL, labelled); property tax NOT duplicated; per-city finance snapshots writing no ledger rows; public debt explicit.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Indicators** (`tools/sim/indicators.py`): GDP (income approach), GDP/capita, unemployment, inflation (price index), productivity, household income, consumer spending, business investment, government revenue, trade balance, poverty, wealth distribution — documented methodology, monthly snapshots, national + per city.
- [x] **Unit 12 — Crises** (`tools/sim/crises.py`): detection from measured conditions only (unemployment/inflation/closures/reserve thresholds in config; scenarios list an empty documented door); propagation via hiring freeze, lending halt, demand factor on consumption + trade; resolution after 2 clean months.
- [x] **Unit 13 — Trade** (`tools/sim/trade.py`): Session 03 flows made physical (real inventory crosses, shortfalls recorded as city events) and fiscal (tariff per flow, keyed by flow id); settlement idempotent per flow; per-city balance from records.
- [x] **Unit 14 — Household Consumption** (`tools/sim/market.py`): real goods from real shelves with fair rationing (equal coverage fraction per city+good); system retailers paid takings (owner shops stay on settlement P&L — no double-count); education/health honestly 0; food hardship hits needs + life events.
- [x] **Unit 15 — Economic Decisions** (`tools/sim/decisions.py`): job change to better vacancies, investing, bank deposits, mortgage purchase — all economy-gated, all logged; business founding reworked (same total cost; part becomes real business capital).
- [x] **Unit 16 — Observatory** (`tools/sim/econview.py`, `tools/sim/__main__.py`): `--economy/--business/--bank/--indicators/--finances` CLI + JSON over live records; data layer only, no fabricated dashboard.

**Session 04 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — all 16 units implemented and test-proven; full suite **223 tests (183 prior + 40 Session 04), all passing**; financial integrity: 0 conservation violations, 0 ledger mismatches, 0 bank reconciliation violations, idempotency/atomicity/append-only/restart all test-proven; economy_sim md5 unchanged (file + output), Godot 8/8, validate_data green (economy data checks added). 242 agents × 90 days = 33.4 s. # Session 05 — Government, Politics & National Administration Engine (owner order "BEGIN SESSION 05", 2026-11-21)

**Gate check:** Session 04 completed + merged (PR #15, main `d38135fb`, 223 tests) before this session began, per the standing gate rule. **Implementation form:** extend the existing systems — the ledger stays the single money spine; SYS-CITY municipal budgets, SYS-BANK, city services, Session 02 meetings/conflicts and Session 04 indicators are reused, never recreated. **Owner's hard rule:** Platform Administrator ≠ Virtual Government — all government authority is in-simulation data (institutions/offices/role checks in sim code); nothing in the sim touches real platform access; stated explicitly in `docs/SESSION-05-REPORT.md`. **Treasury question (deferred by Session 04):** answered — municipal treasuries split from SYS-CITY via a conservation-preserving opening transfer from Session 03's municipal budget records + monthly located-revenue allocation; conservation identity test-pinned. Full detail: `docs/SESSION-05-REPORT.md`.

- [x] **Unit 01 — National Government Foundation** (`tools/sim/government.py`, `data/government-config.json`): one real country (Bangladesh, from citizens' nationality data), both cities, 15 institutions, leadership offices, terms, governance records; multi-country data model proven by a minimal second record (no fabricated nation).
- [x] **Unit 02 — Government Organization**: executive/legislative/judicial branches, ministries, EC, Audit Office, two municipal administrations — defined authority lists enforced by `require_authority`; cross-branch refusals test-pinned.

Full cycles in tests AND the 390-day scenario (months 1 and 13).
- [x] **Unit 05 — Citizen Participation**: petitions/complaints/consultations as feedback records; consultations gate decision-engine policies; opinion + approval computed from measured experiences (ledger wages, employment, prices, services, incidents, crises), snapshotted monthly.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Policies** (`tools/sim/policies.py`): policy objects with budget/duration/expected-vs-measured outcomes; hooks change real behaviour (employment → civil-service vacancies; welfare → poverty-line top-ups; housing → Session 03 construction; infrastructure → road repairs; health → funded doctor investment); spend = exact ledger sum per policy.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Government Budget**: annual cycle (close at months 12/24 with measured actuals; forecast from trailing recorded revenue; configured allocation shares; Speaker/PM approval recorded); overdraft formalised into explicit SYS-BANK loans via Session 04 machinery (sovereign credit on recorded revenue); public debt explicit + implicit, never double-counted.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Public Administration** (`tools/sim/admin.py`): civil servants are real citizens hired via the labour machinery (credential gates intact) into institutions; treasury payroll in the settlement pre-pass, taxed on receipt; evaluations from measured institutional output; promotions/transfers recorded.

- [x] **Unit 10 — Judiciary**: courts with citizen judges (authority-checked); cases only from real disputes (Session 02 escalated conflicts via police referral, Session 04 loan defaults, wage shortfalls); evidence = the underlying records; allegation stored distinctly from finding; remedies through the ledger keeping the loan book consistent; one appeal by the loser, stricter standard, refunds on overturn.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Law Enforcement & Public Safety** (`tools/sim/safety.py`): police institutions staffed by police_officer citizens; incidents ONLY from sim records (escalated conflicts, defaults, measured storm×degraded-road risk) — no arbitrary crime generation; report → investigation by a named officer → court referral/closure; safety stats from records feed opinion.
- [x] **Unit 12 — Corruption & Accountability** (`tools/sim/integrity.py`): monthly audits re-run the real ledger reconciliation over government accounts; spending records queryable via the ledger; conflict-of-interest flags from the office-holder × business-owner data join; integrity index from measured discrepancies only; whistleblower reports investigated by ledger re-check (discipline requires a measured discrepancy).

# Session 06 — Education, Healthcare & Human Development Engine (owner spec received 2026-11-21, docs/specs/SESSION-06-SPEC.md)

**Gate check:** Session 05 completed + merged (PR #16, main `9c1eac90`, 267 tests) before this session began, per the standing gate rule; the owner pre-authorised sequential use of the Session 06–10 specs. **Implementation form:** extend the existing systems — Session 02 enrolment IS the student record, GDD-11 caps stay contract, GDD-12's condition design is implemented (not duplicated), care/education stay civic and unpriced at point of use, no medicine price invented (fictional inventory quantities + internal procurement rates only), institutions/facilities only where real buildings exist. - [x] **Unit 01 — Education framework + institutions** (`tools/sim/institutions.py`, `data/education-health-config.json`): configurable framework as data (sectors, calendar, curricula with subjects/objectives/skills/difficulty/prerequisites); registry of exactly the 4 institutions whose buildings exist (District School, District College, College Library, Shibnagar Primary School); capacities, departments, monthly civic budget authority, measured performance. Types without buildings (kindergarten, university…) are framework-only — never fabricated.
- [x] **Unit 02 — Teachers** (`institutions.py`): real teacher citizens assigned monthly by residence city (college lecturer posts first, 1 per 10 enrolled college students); evaluations computed from measured examination means + attendance (documented skill fallback pre-exam); salaries through the existing payroll — the module moves no money.

- [x] **Unit 04 — Examinations, certifications, dropout** (`academics.py`): batch term examinations; every score a pure function of stored measured inputs + seeded jitter, ALL inputs (jitter included) stored on the result row — recomputation test-pinned; certifications only when measured exam history supports them (completion without certificate is a distinct, kept fact); dropout only from measured circumstances (household debt + below-pass performance; measured low attendance), never a dice roll.
- [x] **Unit 05 — Careers link**: college credential → Session 04 labour-gate eligibility proven end-to-end in the seeded acceptance world (graduate eligible, non-graduate refused); GDD-11 skill caps untouched.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Health profiles + condition layer** (`tools/sim/health.py`): GDD-12 implemented — band + CLOSED cause list (validated at construction) + course + memory; treated/untreated windows, complications only when untreated; effects via rest bend (study blocks now bend too), the emotions 'illness' profile, healthcare need, memories; elder-serious mortality hook in `lifecycle.py` (×3.0 configured, exactly 1.0 without an open serious condition or the layer); monthly health profiles by documented formula; chronic (noncommunicable) onset for ages 50+.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Healthcare network + patient journeys** (`tools/sim/healthcare.py`): exactly the 2 real clinics; staff = real doctor citizens (no doctors → patients wait on the untreated course, capacity falls to base — no conjured staff); one recorded queue: registration → assessment (named doctor, diagnosis roll vs measured skill; failure lengthens recovery + follow-up) → treatment → closure; serious cases stabilised + referred off-district (admission records), never treated in an invented ward.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Medication, pharmacy + budgets** (`healthcare.py`): FICTIONAL inventory (invented names), quantities per facility; monthly procurement SYS-CITY → SYS-MARKET through the ledger with transfer keys + event refs, capped by the month's budget authority (`healthcare_budgets`: allocated vs procured, test-pinned ≤); stockouts leave visits waiting — availability measurably affects care; no patient is ever charged.
- [x] **Unit 09 — Disease simulation** (`tools/sim/disease.py`): one fictional disease construct, configurable parameters; transmission over real contact structure only (household co-residents + seeded institution-mate sample); scheduled recovery, recorded immunity, vaccination effectiveness resolved per citizen on a seeded stream; index cases from the labelled endemic chance + deterministic configured scenarios (standard config: none authored); monthly surveillance per city with threshold-triggered vaccination campaigns bounded by vaccine stock. Labelled simplified — never a medical prediction.
- [x] **Unit 10 — Life-stage care + wellbeing** (`healthcare.py`, `tools/sim/wellbeing.py`): birth life-events → maternal + infant care records (maternal kit consumed when stocked); chronic elders → monthly review visits; enrolled children → term health checks — all through the real facility queue. Wellbeing snapshots computed only from Session 01 emotions + Session 02 social state + needs; no diagnoses.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Research ecosystem** (`tools/sim/research.py`): projects at the District College led by real lecturers, funded from a configured 15% share of the college's budget authority, 3–6 month durations, completions write publication records and raise the college's measured research capability (feeding institution performance).
- [x] **Unit 12 — Human development indicators** (`tools/sim/humandev.py`): literacy (documented proxy), education access, graduation rate, skill distribution, healthcare access + capacity, population health, research activity, life expectancy (observed + model 65.61 from lifecycle's own curve), HDI as the geometric mean of health/education/income indices from Session 04's GDP — a component that cannot be computed is absent, never estimated (no-GDP world → no HDI, test-pinned).
- [x] **Unit 13 — Observatory** (`tools/sim/humandevview.py`, `tools/sim/__main__.py`): `--education/--health/--development` CLI + JSON over real records; data layer only.

# Session 07 — Transportation, Mobility & Smart Infrastructure Engine (owner spec received 2026-11-21, docs/specs/SESSION-07-SPEC.md)

**Gate check:** Session 06 completed + merged (PR #17, main `1bce13dd`, 325 tests) before this session began, per the standing gate rule; the owner pre-authorised sequential use of the Session 06–10 specs. **Implementation form:** extend, never recreate — Session 03's road graph IS the network, citizens' schedules ARE the trip demand, Session 04's supply orders ARE the freight demand, Session 06's referrals ARE the emergency demand. No invented prices (fuel unpriced — quantities only; bus/rickshaw/ferry covered by the existing monthly transport line); traffic only from simulated movement. Full detail: `docs/SESSION-07-REPORT.md`.

- [x] **Unit 01 — Vehicle registry** (`tools/sim/transport.py`, `vehicles` table): persistent vehicles (type from vehicles.json, owner citizen/business/SYS-MARKET, zone, fuel, odometer, condition, status); genesis fleets grounded in the world — buses at real terminals, trucks for producing businesses, 2 carrier trucks/city, rented cabs for employed taxi drivers (matching the settlement's existing 200 NCr/day rental).
- [x] **Unit 02 — Vehicle purchase + ownership** (`transport.py` monthly sweep): employed adults buy at prices.json prices through the ledger (affordability ladder bicycle → premium car; transfer keys; taxi drivers excluded — they rent); vehicles relocate with their owners' journeys.
- [x] **Unit 03 — Trip generation from real demand** (`transport.py` daily pass): consecutive schedule blocks in different zones become recorded journeys (origin, destination, purpose, depart/arrive, route, distance, fare, fuel); Session 06 patient visits generate same-day healthcare trips; migration records generate the household head's intercity move trip.
- [x] **Unit 04 — Mode choice** (`transport.py`): own vehicle (by vehicles.json road rights) → walk ≤1,200 m → bus (route + hours) → rickshaw ≤3,500 m → taxi (city service exists + balance covers the fare) → walk; intercity: own/cab over the bridge → ferry (ghat-to-ghat only, in hours) → intercity bus.
- [x] **Unit 05 — Traffic + congestion** (`transport.py`, `road_loads`): the day's vehicle trips tallied per (segment, hour) in PCE; factor `1 + 3.0 × flow/capacity` (SIM MODEL) capped 3.0 against roads.py capacities; today's times use today's factors, tomorrow's routing uses today's peak factors; statistics computed only from trip records.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Road state in routing** (`transport.py` Network): Session 03 condition slows travel (Session 05 repairs speed it back up); condition < 20 or `road_segments.closed` = impassable, routes detour (test-proven).
- [x] **Unit 07 — Public transit** (`transit_routes`, `transit_runs`): Rupsha City Loop + Shibnagar Town Bus as stop cycles over the graph on bus road rights; headways/hours/capacity from config + vehicles.json; boardings per trip, runs recorded with boardings vs capacity, over-capacity spill to rickshaw/walk on record; intercity bus over RC-ROAD-1.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Taxi service + fares** (`transport.py`): fares at prices.json taxi_fares (base + per-km, its own rain/night surge) charged rider → SYS-MARKET per trip with transfer keys + day event refs; unpaid (insufficient funds) recorded, never silently free; driver income stays the settlement wage (no double count).
- [x] **Unit 09 — Freight physical layer** (`freight_shipments`): every Session 04 supply order + intercity trade flow writes a shipment (business/city endpoints, truck, route, congested arrival, delay); daily pass flips arrived shipments to delivered; Session 04's money and inventory contract untouched (boundary stated in the report).
- [x] **Unit 10 — Fuel, maintenance, lifecycle** (`vehicle_events`): fuel consumed per distance from vehicles.json rates, deducted from tanks; refuel events below reserve (quantities only — no fuel price exists); monthly distance-based wear + service events; cabs returned when a driver leaves the profession.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Emergency mobility** (`transport.py`): Session 06 off-district referral admissions create same-day ambulance trips (facility zone → city gate, priority factor 0.5 SIM MODEL, destination `off_district`, admission id on record; no ambulance class exists in vehicles.json so no vehicle row is invented).
- [x] **Unit 12 — Intercity + ferry** (`transport.py`): bridge journeys over Session 03's RC-ROAD-1 with its +1.5 km convention (matching Session 04's); RC-FERRY-1 serves ghat-to-ghat journeys at the config crossing time (SIM MODEL — the ghats share no coordinate frame).
- [x] **Unit 13 — Transport economics** (`transport.py` monthly): municipal transit subsidy in government worlds (SIM MODEL 15 NCr/boarding, capped, balance-guarded, from `SYS-TREASURY-<city>`); vehicle purchases + taxi fares already ledger flows; monthly `transport_statistics` snapshots per city + national (trips by mode, distance, boardings, fares, fuel, emissions, shipments, delays, max congestion).
- [x] **Unit 14 — Environmental outputs**: per-trip fuel + emissions (config SIM MODEL factors per fuel type) recorded and aggregated monthly — the designed feed into Session 08.
- [x] **Unit 15 — Observatory** (`tools/sim/transportview.py`, `tools/sim/__main__.py`): `--transport` CLI + JSON over real records (vehicles, trips, fares, congestion hotspots, transit routes/loads, freight, statistics, methodology incl. the rail/air position); data layer only.
- [x] **Unit 16 — Rail/air/water position (honest)**: NO rail/air/maritime operations — a station plot and a launch terminal exist as buildings but no track/airfield/port data exists anywhere and none was fabricated; the river ferry runs because Session 03 persisted RC-FERRY-1 and both ghats are real buildings. Stated plainly in the report.

**Session 07 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — all areas implemented and test-proven; full suite **352 tests (325 prior + 27 Session 07), all passing** (per-module sweep of all 59 test modules — this VM is heavily CPU-shared, so the recorded evidence is the complete per-module sweep plus the acceptance module's own long-run file); the spec's 12 acceptance criteria proven in 200-day worlds incl. restart identity + determinism with the layer live; congestion proven two ways (organic max factor 1.0814 at 242 agents; corridor load test factor > 1.1 with slower travel + closure detours); economy_sim md5 unchanged (file + output), Godot 8/8, validate_data green (transport-config checks added). Benchmark 242 × 90 days = 98.9 s: 85,189 trips (~947/day), 12,364 bus boardings, 3,398 taxi trips (349,256 NCr fares), 56 organic vehicle purchases (1,379,000 NCr), 332 shipments (195 delivered by day 90), transit subsidy 175,695 NCr, 0 ledger mismatches. Honest limits: ferry boardings 0 organically (no schedule produces a ghat-to-ghat OD pair — machinery test-proven); organic congestion modest against arterial capacities; freight arrival does not re-time Session 04's inventory settlement (deliberate boundary); no signal/queue/incident model (no data). # Session 08 — Weather, Climate, Environment & Ecosystem Intelligence Engine (owner spec received 2026-11-21, docs/specs/SESSION-08-SPEC.md)

**Gate check:** Session 07 completed + merged (PR #18, main `9f5d5806`, 352 tests) before this session began, per the standing gate rule; the owner pre-authorised sequential use of the Session 06–10 specs. **Implementation form:** extend, never recreate — the engine's daily weather draw IS Rupsha's regional weather, Session 03's terrain/infrastructure/buildings/roads ARE the exposure and drainage, Session 04's market IS the farm outlet, Session 05's law book + treasuries ARE the governance and response funds, Session 07's trips + emissions statistics ARE the evacuation record and pollution source. All conditions SIMULATED, labelled, never presented as observations or forecasts. Full detail: `docs/SESSION-08-REPORT.md`.

- [x] **Unit 01 — Regional weather engine** (`tools/sim/environment.py`, `data/environment-config.json`): per-city daily weather (temperature, humidity, pressure, wind, cloud, precipitation mm, visibility, preset) — Rupsha = the engine's existing draw (unchanged); Shibnagar = seeded Markov reweighting with season/region rain factors + wet/dry persistence; persisted in `env_weather`, restart-proof.
- [x] **Unit 02 — Seasons + day/night**: six-season Bangladeshi calendar as config tiling the 365-day year exactly once (validate_data-guarded); seasonal temperature/rain/daylight; sunrise/sunset as documented approximation recorded per weather row.
- [x] **Unit 03 — Water systems**: per-city catchment — rainfall → Session 03 drainage capacity → runoff → river (recession toward base), infiltration → groundwater (resident draw), surface storage + evaporation; monthly water availability per city.
- [x] **Unit 04 — Flooding (mechanism)**: river ≥ flood level floods terrain-exposed parcels/zones — roads close (`road_segments.closed`, Session 07 detours), buildings lose condition, businesses record disruption, households displaced by record, residents' needs impacted; episode ends on recession + hysteresis.
- [x] **Unit 05 — Agriculture**: crop cycles (aman/boro/winter vegetables) on the real agricultural parcels; growth from soil moisture + fertility + recorded rainfall; harvest into Session 04's market via `adjust_inventory` kind 'harvest'; failure by record under drought/flood stress; no crop price invented.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Soil**: per-parcel moisture (rain-fed) + fertility (cropping drain, fallow recovery, flood erosion), SIM MODEL labelled.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Vegetation + wildlife indices**: cover index per habitat region (growth/drought/deforestation from recorded construction/conservation bonus); wildlife population INDEX drifting toward cover × water availability — abstracted, labelled, no biology claims.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Pollution (traceable)**: monthly air/water indices per city with stored source breakdowns (Session 07 emissions kg, active industry, Session 03 wastewater/drainage overloads, flood episodes); air above threshold measurably hits residents' healthcare need.
- [x] **Unit 09 — Disaster scenarios (configured only)**: six definitions in config, NONE armed by default; the only door is `trigger_scenario()` (unknown ids raise); cyclone/flood-surge/drought/heatwave/severe-storm effects run through the real weather/water/damage machinery; each event records extent/intensity/duration/affected/damage.
- [x] **Unit 10 — Disaster response + recovery (actual resources)**: relief per displaced household from the real municipal treasury balance through the ledger (keyed + event_ref), shelter = a real civic building in a safe zone (or recorded unavailable), evacuation trips in Session 07's trips table, medical flag via Session 06 profiles; treasury-funded repairs restore recorded damage; `recovered` only when relief paid + damage repaired; roads reopen.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Environmental governance**: Session 05 law book extended with the `emission_limit` kind (+ constitutional bounds) — enacted laws measurably cut the industry pollution component and speed vegetation recovery (test-proven both ways); no law = exactly the unregulated model.
- [x] **Unit 12 — Climate trends + scenarios**: monthly `env_snapshots` per city (temperature, rainfall, water end-state, availability, vegetation, pollution, vulnerability, active scenario name); climate scenario deltas (baseline/warming_drier/warming_wetter) applied transparently and recorded.
- [x] **Unit 13 — Effects on people**: flood/heatwave needs impacts on real residents; polluted-air healthcare impact; farm output feeds Session 04's food chain; no parallel human system.
- [x] **Unit 14 — Observatory** (`tools/sim/environmentview.py`, `tools/sim/__main__.py`): `--environment` CLI + JSON over real records only, carrying the simulated-not-observed note; data layer only.

**Session 08 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — all spec areas implemented and test-proven; full suite **377 tests (352 prior + 25 Session 08), all passing** (per-module sweep of all 64 test modules — this VM is heavily CPU-shared, so the recorded evidence is the complete per-module sweep plus the acceptance module's own long-run file); the spec's 14 acceptance criteria proven (370-day acceptance world: full year of weather for both regions, monsoon rain > winter rain per city, 20+ trend snapshots, crop cycles with outcomes, pollution rows with source breakdowns, flood-surge scenario + ledger integrity, restart identity + determinism with the layer live). Scenario (100 agents × 210 days, cyclone at day 150): cyclone recovered — 33 building-damage impacts, 3 households displaced, relief 9,000 NCr, repairs 52,800 NCr, 3 evacuation trips; 4 organic monsoon flood episodes; 87.36 produce lots harvested; 0 ledger mismatches. Benchmark 242 × 90 days = 76.5 s. economy_sim md5 unchanged (file + output), Godot 8/8, validate_data green (environment-config checks added). Honest limits: monsoon rainfall totals run high (labelled SIM MODEL, not calibrated); all farmland is Shibnagar's 3 parcels (the only agricultural parcels in the data); recovery is resource-bound — an empty treasury stalls repairs by design; earthquake/landslide/wildfire/tsunami have no authored scenarios (no honest exposure basis in this delta terrain); vegetation/wildlife are indices, not ecology. Report: `docs/SESSION-08-REPORT.md`. **Session 08's gate obligations are met; Session 09 has NOT been started.**

## Session 09 — Advanced Autonomous AI Civilization & Collective Intelligence

A coordination layer over Sessions 01–08 (audit first: citizens/business/government/cities/disasters already decide — recreate nothing). No LLM calls anywhere; deterministic rules over measured state only.

- [x] **Unit 01 — Agent registry + permissions**: registry VIEW over real actors (citizen/business/institution/city/government/system), capability config (only government holds `enact_policy`), bounded recorded params (`agent_params`/`agent_learning`); denials are recorded failed actions.
- [x] **Unit 02 — Extended personal goals**: priority/progress/feasibility/deadline/completion-rule on `character_goals` + progress log; derivation from measured state; completion/abandonment by record; monthly step = a real bank deposit; priority adaptation as bounded learning.
- [x] **Unit 03 — Multi-level memory**: family/organisation/community/civilization levels over real event tables, dedupe by source ref, importance-ranked counted retrieval, bounded compression; retrieval provably changes a decision (family business-failure memory cuts a derived business goal's priority by exactly 30).
- [x] **Unit 04 — Wage negotiation**: one structured protocol at hiring (premium from measured leverage × learned factor; employer cap + S04 solvency; counter/floor/walk-away; every offer persisted; both sides learn). Buyer/seller price negotiation scoped OUT (S04 prices are per city+good — no per-seller price exists); supplier selection upgraded instead (recorded orders + measured reliability ranking).
- [x] **Unit 05 — Business decision records**: every executed supply order carries an inputs → rule → outcome explanation in `agent_decisions`.
- [x] **Unit 06 — Assessments (closed loop)**: monthly per-city problem detection from measured indicators → funded action linked (S05 policy / S03 project / emission law / coordination) → same indicator re-measured and evaluated; pollution recommends to the government agent by message.
- [x] **Unit 07 — Forecasts**: transparent trend models (population/housing vacancy/congestion/business profit) stored with inputs, scored against actuals when due.
- [x] **Unit 08 — Emergency coordination**: task sequence over Session 08 disasters, completion proven by S08's own response records, overdue escalation by message, unmet-at-recovery closes as recorded failure + review task.
- [x] **Unit 09 — Conflict resolution + orchestration**: labour contention closure (`contention_lost` + recorded rule; household unit contention remains S02's first-mover rule); engine hooks (daily coordination when a disaster is live, monthly pass last); `--agents` observatory CLI.

**Session 09 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — the coordination layer implemented and test-proven; full suite **406 tests (377 prior + 29 Session 09), all passing** (per-module sweep of all 72 test modules); the spec's 17 acceptance criteria mapped to evidence in `test_session09_acceptance.py` (180-day world, registry identity across reopen, restart identity 45+45 ≡ 90, determinism with the layer live, household contention, permissions enforced in-world). Scenario (100 agents × 210 days, cyclone day 150): 888 supply orders with decision explanations, 36 forecasts scored (mean pct error 0.0382), the assessment loop fired organically end-to-end (identified → acted → evaluated), 28 coordination tasks done, 103 goals completed + 296 goal steps, 355 learning records, memories family 115 / organisation 488 / community 48 / civilization 9, 0 ledger mismatches. Benchmark 242 × 90 days = 79.8 s (Session 08: 76.5 s). economy_sim md5 unchanged (file + output), Godot 8/8, validate_data green (agents-config checks added). Honest limits: **zero organic wage negotiations in every long run** — measured leverage (financial-need satisfaction + score margin over the pool) rarely crosses the premium threshold, so hiring proceeds at the posted wage; the protocol is unit-proven (ask/counter/walk-away + learning) and integration-proven, and the threshold is an owner tuning question; supplier *price* negotiation does not exist (S04 prices are per city+good) — reliability ranking is the honest signal; learning is bounded parameter adaptation only, no self-modifying code; assessments link actions, they do not take them. Report: `docs/SESSION-09-REPORT.md`. **Session 09's gate obligations are met; Session 10 has NOT been started.**

- [x] **Unit 10 — Endurance + benchmarks**: 242 × 365 days full battery green; scale probe (target 500 → 342 actual, housing-stock cap); recovery timing; checkpoint timings. `tools/run_session10_endurance.py`.
- [x] **Unit 11 — Architecture + checklist**: `docs/FINAL-ARCHITECTURE.md` (15 layers → real modules/tables/CLIs + boundaries); the spec's 20-item checklist in the report §8: **19 PROVEN, 1 PARTIALLY PROVEN** (secure data access, local scope), 0 unverified claims.

**Session 10 result:** COMPLETE 2026-11-21 — **the 10-session programme is COMPLETE.** Full suite **438 tests (406 prior + 32 Session 10), all passing** (per-module sweep of all 78 modules, 0 failed). Endurance (242 × 365 d): 500.5 s, population 242 → 264, 34,323 ledger rows reconciled, 0 mismatches / 0 conservation violations, 12/12 audits clean, 5,475 registry events, DB 2.17 → 213.6 MB, peak memory 110.6 MB, checkpoint save 2.90 s / restore 0.79 s. Scale probe 342 × 90 d = 86.9 s clean (genesis capped at 342 by the housing stock — measured, not hidden). Recovery: killed day 41 → reopen + finish 7.9 s, digest == twin. economy_sim md5 unchanged, Godot 8/8, validate_data green. Honest limits: one country/two cities; technology levels 0.0 in unseeded worlds (no organic college enrolment — derivation + gate proven seeded); no real-time server; UE/3D verification remains the owner's PC milestone (Phase 1; Step 0 PC specs still owed). - [x] **Unit DP** — Lost-Item Hand-Back Journey (DF/DJ follow-up): DF's return is a direct write — no NPC carries the item back. Design the hand-back: who carries a found item to its owner (finder vs a civic desk), where the hand-back happens (the `lost_anchor` vs the owner's home), and what the §3.1 favour records when the owner is unreachable (household moved, owner deceased).   - **Done:** finder carries to the owner's current home; civic desk rejected (no building/staff/source); moved = re-route; deceased = orphaned favour (no hand-back to the heir — `die()` moves money+property, never inventory); no new field; found-item re-loss edge recorded (Queue 19). 23 checks, 0 failures.
- [x] **Unit DQ** — Seeded-Row Condition & Decay (DJ follow-up): seeded furniture/tool rows have no condition — a worn tool cannot break or be repaired, and a damaged piece of furniture never degrades. Design the condition model: a 0–100 condition on furniture/tool rows, what lowers it (use, weather, moves), what a broken tool does to the owner's profession work, and how repair is priced (from `prices.json` where a price exists; unpriced where none does — no invented prices). Design only. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.3 (DJ), `data/furniture.json`, `data/tools.json`, `data/prices.json`.
  - **Done:** condition 0–100 in state JSON, seeded 100; wear −1/work-day (tools), −2/move, −1/exposed day; thresholds adopted from vehicles (service 60, restored 90); broken tool refuses its work blocks (wage consequence honestly deferred to the labour settlement); broken furniture drops the effective GDD-10 tier; repair unpriced (no repair key in `prices.json`), replacement at catalogue price. 24 checks, 0 failures.

Design the display: the character panel's credential row while its document row is `lost` (label, icon state, and what the panel must NOT claim), following GDD-05 panel patterns. Design only; no sim code changes. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.3 (DN), GDD-05, `data/documents.json`.
  - **Done:** Credentials block on the Skills tab; chips "Paper lost — credential valid" / "Found — awaiting hand-back"; dashed outline + badge, never a red X; never revoked/invalid/expired/missing; recovery path is the §3.1 favour; deed/licence out of scope. 23 checks, 0 failures.
- [x] **Unit DS** — Mission-Progress Regression Suite (DL follow-up): the DL writer is proven only for mission 1.4 in its own tests — there is no standing regression proving the DA gate opens/closes per mission as new missions arrive. Build the suite: a verify script + tests asserting, for every mission with a §3.6 condition, that the writer completes it exactly when its condition holds and the DA gate stays closed otherwise; the suite must fail loudly when a new mission ships without a condition. Verify script + tests; no sim behaviour change. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §2, §3.6 (DG/DL), `tools/sim/missions.py`, `tools/sim/photographs.py`.
  - **Done:** `CONDITIONED_MISSIONS` registry in `missions.py` (1.4, 2.1); `tools/sim/tests/test_mission_progress_regression.py` (13 tests: registry tripwire both directions, writer-completes per mission, gate stays closed otherwise) + verify script; 1.3/2.4 recorded out of scope (trees, no condition, no verifier). 19 checks, 0 failures; no sim behaviour change.

Wire it: the environment pass's exposure (flood / cyclone / severe_storm over `exposed_zones`) applies −1 to the tools of characters whose home stands in an exposed zone, beside the furniture wear, with the `tool_broken` event firing when a tool reaches 0 the same way work wear fires it. Implement `repair_tool()` / `repair_furniture()` in `tools/sim/lost_items.py`: restore the row's condition to 90 (`RESTORED_CONDITION`), refuse a row already at 90+ or at 100, refuse an unknown row; no ledger charge and no invented price. Sim change + tests. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.3 (DQ), `tools/sim/lost_items.py`, `data/prices.json`.
- [x] **Unit EI** — Lost-Piece vs Effective-Tier Decision (EF follow-up): EF records as open, not decided, whether a lost furniture piece drops the effective tier (a lost piece still counts as standing today because its row stands and the hand-back returns it). Decide it, record the reasoning in GDD-04 §3.3 + GDD-10 §5, and implement the decision in `effective_furniture_tier()` with tests proving both the lost-piece case and the broken-piece case. Design decision + sim change + tests. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.3 (EF), docs/GDD-10-HOUSING-AND-HOME-LIFE.md §5, `tools/sim/lost_items.py`.

- [x] **Unit EK** — Deceased-Giver Favour Guard (DP follow-up): DP requires the generator never to offer a lost-item favour for a deceased giver, and no code enforces it. Enforce it at the favour-instance creation path: `create_favour_instance()` refuses when the giver is deceased (a deceased giver cannot offer a favour), with tests proving the refusal and that a living giver is unaffected. Sim change + tests. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.1/§3.3 (DP), `tools/sim/missions.py`.
- [x] **Unit EL** — Broken-Tool Wage Consequence (DQ/EE follow-up): DQ honestly defers the wage consequence of a broken tool's bent work blocks (the labour settlement has no absence docking) and EE leaves the deferral in place. - [x] **Unit EM** — F2/F3 Furniture Purchase Point (DQ follow-up): furniture tiers F2/F3 have no purchase event — a household can never reach them, so the effective tier caps at F1 in practice. Specify the purchase point (where F2/F3 pieces are bought, at catalogue price from `data/furniture.json`, through the existing purchase event or a new one — no invented prices, no new currency) in GDD-10 §5 + GDD-04 §3.3, with the sim wiring if the purchase event exists. Design + data; sim change only where the event already exists. Cross-refs: docs/GDD-10-HOUSING-AND-HOME-LIFE.md §5, `data/furniture.json`, `data/prices.json`.
- [x] **Unit EN** — Property Deed Seeding (DN follow-up): no purchase event seeds a property deed — a bought home has no deed row. Specify the deed seeding (the deed row written at the purchase event, mirroring the uniform/tool issue-at-hiring rule: issued, not bought, no ledger charge) in GDD-04 §3.3 + GDD-10, with the sim wiring if the purchase event exists. Design + data; sim change only where the event already exists. Cross-refs: GDD-04 §3.3 (DN), docs/GDD-10-HOUSING-AND-HOME-LIFE.md, `tools/sim/lost_items.py`.

Run the seeded engine month, read the actual storm days and worked days from `instantiated_schedules` for the construction workers, extract one worker's real daily balance path for the storm month, and compare it with the tool's storm-position model at the measured position (days 3 and 22): does the tool's −150 entry dip match the settlement's actual dip, and where do they differ (second earner, arrears, other income)? Record the comparison as an addendum to `docs/ECONOMY-SIM-REPORT-V7.md`. Analysis + data; no tuning call. Cross-refs: GDD-03 §2, `tools/sim/labour.py`, `tools/economy_sim.py`, `docs/ECONOMY-SIM-REPORT-V7.md`.
- [x] **Unit GD** — Two-Earner Storm-Position Scenario (FB follow-up): the tool still models a single-earner household on the base table's costs — a household with a second earner dips less, and the V7 report records that as open. Extend the storm-position scenario to a two-earner household: the second earner is a day-wage construction worker on the same storm days (same `storm_days`, same pay rule), and the household costs follow the engine's own scaling (`engine._living_costs` for two adults — food/clothing scaled, the rest flat), not an invented line. Measure the month-1 minbal per position for the two-earner household and record it beside the single-earner figures. Analysis + data; no tuning call. Cross-refs: GDD-03 §2, `tools/sim/labour.py`, `tools/economy_sim.py`, `tools/sim/engine.py`.
- [x] **Unit GE** — Storm-Position Bridge Line (FB follow-up): the V7 report states the bridge observation in prose (entry starting balance 5,000 + worst dip −500 = 4,500) but the tool's printed bridge check still uses the base table's zero-start dip only. Wire the worst storm-position dip into the bridge check as a printed line (entry rows: starting balance + worst storm-position minbal), with a test pinning the line, so the storm bridge is part of the tool's output rather than a report-only observation. Sim change + tests; the pinned md5 moves again (v6 kept as history). Cross-refs: GDD-03 §2, `tools/economy_sim.py`, `tools/sim/tests/test_no_regression.py`.

Measure the settlement's factor on a real month (rain/storm-day counts from a seeded run, per-earner gross with and without the factor) and decide whether the tool models it or records the difference — no invented counts, no tuning call. Analysis + data; the pinned md5 moves only if a modelled change is made. Cross-refs: GDD-03 §8, `tools/economy_sim.py`, `tools/sim/engine.py`, `docs/ECONOMY-SIM-REPORT-V7.md` §15. **Unit KC DONE 2026-11-24 (63 checks, 0 failures):** DECIDED — model it. The EY month's weather (seed `ey-measure`: rain day_index 2 and 28, storm 3 and 22) is 2 rain days + 2 storm days, factor exactly 1.05 — all six taxi drivers in that world settled at band × 1.05 (14,700 / 24,150 / 37,800), including the Priya-household taxi earner, so the surge applies to a second earner exactly as to a single earner. `simulate(..., taxi_rain_days=..., taxi_storm_days=...)` scales the taxi gross by the settlement's formula, tax and uplift recomputed on the surged gross, rental stays the flat 5,200; refused on any non-taxi row and on counts outside one 30-day month. Single-earner nets +455 / +691 / +1,080 (renting entry 1,300 → 1,755); the surge shrinks both early dips but closes neither (HD senior −72 → −42, KA renting senior −56 → −9). The bridge check stays flat — the factor is ≥ 1, so the flat lines are the conservative case. md5 KA `5f50931c…` → `f6cec5a2…` (KA kept as history); 6 new tests pin the figures + refusals + printed section. Honest limit: the settlement applies the surge only in the SYS-EMPLOYERS branch — a business-employed taxi driver settles unsurged — a code read (the EY world holds none), recorded open as a settlement question (Queue 30 Unit KF).
